Inside this border are three red-ink seals: one large square seal at the top, and two smaller seals (one square, one vertical rectangle) positioned side-by-side at the bottom. A large, irregular brownish water stain covers the left half of the left-hand page.</image-desc>

-><insert>儀征厲氏家藏</insert><-

-><insert>唫翯婆娑</insert> <insert>獵百城</insert><-

<vocab>儀征厲氏 (Yizheng Li-shi), 厲鶚 (Li E), 藏書印 (collector's seal), 獵百城 (Liebaicheng), 唫翯婆娑 (Jinhe Posuo)</vocab>

--- Page 2 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<header>天　　地</header>
<warning>Water staining and foxing are present across both pages, but the text on the left page remains clearly legible.</warning>

<image-desc>A circular illustration centered within the top half of the framed page. It depicts a green, forested mountain or island rising from turbulent, stylized blue and white waves. Swirls of mist or vapor rise from the base of the mountain, and a pale yellow-green sky fills the upper portion of the circle.</image-desc>

---

<margin>
朱文公曰
</margin>

> 惟天爲大惟君最尊政教兆
> 於人理祥變見於天文行有
> 玷玦則曰象顯示天有妖孽
> 則德宜日新確乎在上而晶
> 明者天之體也魄乎在下而
> 安靜者地之形也

<vocab>朱文公, 政教, 天文, 玷玦, 妖孽, 日新, 天之體, 地之形</vocab>

--- Page 3 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

<header>天色忽變占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting rugged mountain peaks and trees under an ominous, dark grey sky. The heavy clouds suggest a sudden atmospheric change, reflecting the subject of the divination title above.</image-desc>

朱文公曰 天色忽變四夷来侵
宋志曰 天色忽變是謂黑常
四夷来侵也
洪範曰 清明者天之體也天
色忽變是謂黑常

<column-break/>

<header>地土忽陷占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting showing green-peaked mountains and a valley with two small buildings. The ground in the foreground near the houses is depicted with dark lines indicating cracks or subsidence, visually representing the "sinking earth" omen.</image-desc>

朱文公曰 地土忽陷萬民離散
宋志曰 地土陷臣下專政萬民
離散亦為失地
京房曰 山無故自下陷天下
兵作
范曄漢書曰
地陷者王室亂大臣
專政

<vocab>地陷, 天色, 朱文公, 宋志, 京房, 范曄, 漢書, 洪範, 專政, 離散, 兵作, 四夷, 黑常</vocab>

--- Page 4 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>
<header>地 震 總 占 | 天 裂 占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape illustration framed within a black border. At the top of the border, the title "地 震 總 占" (General Omens of Earthquakes) is inscribed. The painting depicts rocky mountains in ink and light blue/brown washes, with sparse trees and two traditional buildings with dark tiled roofs. Below the image is a block of vertical Chinese text.</image-desc>

朱文公曰 地震者謂陰有餘臣奪君權而兵興
宋志曰 地震動是謂臣強陽伏不能
出陰迫而不能入陰有餘也若外戚擅
權后妃專政則土為之變異亦人同下
有謀及民擾則地震動其分兵亂大
占其時春則歲不昌夏乃君憂
五穀傷秋則兵起冬乃邑有土功
春秋傳曰伯陽甫曰 天地之氣不適
其序久之亂也陽伏而不出陰迫而
不能入於是有地震

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape illustration framed within a black border. At the top of the border, the title "天 裂 占" (Omens of Heaven Splitting) is inscribed. The painting shows green and brown mountain peaks under a pale sky. A large, curved, white arc representing a rift in the sky spans across the landscape. Below the image is a block of vertical Chinese text, sparsely arranged.</image-desc>

朱文公曰 天裂是謂陽不足君
弱臣亂而天裂
宋志曰 天裂是謂陽不足臣
亂而君失道土地將裂
班固漢書曰
天裂陽不足皆為臣下
強盛將害上之變也

<vocab>地震 (Earthquake), 天裂 (Heavenly rift), 朱文公 (Zhu Xi), 宋志 (Song Annals), 班固漢書 (History of Han by Ban Gu), 伯陽甫 (Bo Yangfu), 陰陽 (Yin and Yang), 兵興 (outbreak of war), 后妃專政 (regency of empress/consorts)</vocab>

<header>地震洪水占 地陷占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting the divination for earthquakes and floods. Raging white waves fill the lower portion of the frame, with the dark roof of a single house visible floating upon them. In the background, craggy mountain peaks rise through a thick layer of mist and clouds.</image-desc>

晉書曰

地震洪水出陰道盛陽之應

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting the divination for land subsidence. At the base of a large, shadowed mountain, traditional buildings are shown sinking into the ground. To the right of the houses stands a tall, monolithic stone tower or pillar on a patch of level ground near a body of water.</image-desc>

范曄曰

地陷者王室亂陷塔者大臣專
政

<header>地震於屋占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting in ink and light color. In the foreground, several small houses with tiled roofs are nestled among trees. In the background, there are rolling hills and a prominent mountain peak rendered with blue and green washes. The water or mist in the distance is suggested by empty space and light brushstrokes.</image-desc>

班固漢書曰
地震於屋瓦落臣下強盛將
動而為害

<column-break/>

<header>地鳴有穀占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting a man in traditional robes standing in an open area, looking toward distant mountains. The scene is atmospheric, with dark, heavy clouds swirling in the sky above the hills. To the left and right are sparse, gnarled trees. The overall mood suggests a significant natural or celestial phenomenon.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
地鳴有穀天子失國政
宋志曰
地鳴有穀天子失國政任
婦女

<vocab>地震, 地鳴, 班固, 漢書, 朱文公, 宋志, 天子, 國政, 婦女</vocab>

--- Page 7 ---
<language>Classical Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>
<header>地震於宗廟占</header>

<image-desc>A woodblock print illustration in ink and light color depicting a courtyard of an ancestral temple ("宗廟") during an earthquake. In the center, an official in a formal red robe and black winged cap kneels on the ground. To the right, a section of a white-walled building with a tiled roof is severely damaged and leaning. A tall, sparsely-leaved tree stands to the left. Mountains and foliage are visible in the background under a light wash of color. The entire scene is enclosed within a rectangular border.</image-desc>

<margin>宋志曰</margin>
地震於宗廟宮室或動而不已者
國有叛臣讒佞進大臣叛動則誅
罰下以理而上下不相親改在女子
京房易傳曰
地震於宗廟者邑有反臣國
有亂臣動不已

<column-break/>

<header>地震災異占</header>

<image-desc>A woodblock print illustration of a desolate natural landscape, representing a disaster omen ("災異"). A large, rounded hill or mound is the central feature on the right side. Several leafless trees are scattered across an uneven terrain rendered with sharp, jagged ink strokes, suggesting ground tremors or cracking. In the foreground, small patches of grass grow on the broken earth. There are no human figures or structures in this scene, which is enclosed in a rectangular border.</image-desc>

<margin>范曄曰</margin>
地陰也應當安靜今其越陰之
職專陽之政故應之以震
京房傳曰
地為大臣之位當安載萬民
懷藏物數今動搖者不為君
安載萬民萬物也

<vocab>地震, 宗廟, 災異, 宋志, 范曄, 京房易傳, 叛臣, 讒佞</vocab>

<header>天裂見人占</header>
<header>地震禽牛馬占</header>

<image-desc>A watercolor illustration of a jagged mountain landscape with small bamboo plants. In the upper left corner, in a pale sky, four human figures in long dark robes are depicted walking in a line. A rocky cliff face rises on the right side.</image-desc>

晉書曰

天裂見人兵起國亡之兆

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A watercolor illustration showing a white horse and a dark brown ox running frantically across a landscape with trees and hills. Small grey brushstrokes near the trees and animals represent falling debris and the movement of the earth, signifying an earthquake.</image-desc>

宋志曰

若牛馬禽獸奔走者黍患之

京房易妖曰

地震牛馬禽獸者天子失位

<vocab>天裂, 兵起, 國亡, 晉書, 地震, 禽獸, 京房易妖, 宋志, 天子, 黍患</vocab>

--- Page 9 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

<header>天雨草占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting in shades of green, blue, and grey. Dark, feathery strokes representing falling grass descend diagonally from the upper left sky toward a cluster of jagged mountains.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
天雨草恠禄信衰所致
宋志曰
天雨草恠禄信衰所致是謂
天失其性天雨赤草其民流
荀悅漢書曰
天雨草臣專政草如菠莎相
繆結如丸彈

<column-break/>

<header>天鳴有聲占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting depicting a stormy, dark grey sky swirling above a hilly terrain. A man dressed in a blue robe stands on a path, looking up at the heavens with one hand raised, as if reacting to a great sound.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
天鳴有聲人主有憂驚
宋志曰
天鳴有聲人主有憂驚百姓勞
失厥土
晋書曰
天鳴有聲陰氣盛陽氣微人
主憂

<vocab>天雨草, 天鳴, 占, 朱文公, 宋志, 晋書, 荀悅, 漢書, 陰陽, 專政</vocab>

--- Page 10 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>
<header>地生毛占</header>

<image-desc>A colored woodblock-style illustration of a rocky mountain landscape. Fine vertical black lines are depicted emerging from the ground among the grass and rocks, representing the phenomenon of "earth growing hair."</image-desc>

朱文公曰
地生毛人勞兵起之徵
宋志曰
地生毛為金失性人將勞
地鏡曰
地若生毛天下亂兵起
晉書曰
金失其性則地生毛人將勞
軍不寧

<column-break/>

<header>天雨石占</header>

<image-desc>A colored woodblock-style illustration showing black stones falling from dark, swirling clouds in the sky. A person in a pink robe is shown in the foreground running toward a mountain for safety. The scene is set in a landscape with jagged rocks and trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
天雨石大戰而君凶
宋志曰
天雨石為信不施而詐妄作其
下君長凶石墜地近尺餘類雞
卵雨頭銳者名天鼓大戰殷討
之末雨石如甕而為武王所伐
范曄漢書曰
天雨石從高及下民困之象

<vocab>地生毛, 天雨石, 朱文公, 宋志, 地鏡, 晉書, 范曄漢書, 占卜, 天鼓, 殷討</vocab>

--- Page 11 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

<header>天 陰 晦 占</header>

<image-desc>An ink wash landscape illustration within a rectangular border. Dark, dense clouds hang low over a range of mountains. Trees and rocky outcrops in the foreground are painted with dark, heavy ink, creating a somber, overcast atmosphere typical of traditional Chinese literati painting.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

天陰晦而不雨者內亂而陰謀圖
議

宋志曰

天忽晝夜陰晦晝不見日夜不
見星月有陰謀一曰為內亂下將
謀上若雨敵相當陰相圖議也

晉書曰

天晝夜陰晦則下有陰謀也

<column-break/>

<header>地 成 泉 占</header>

<image-desc>An ink wash landscape illustration within a rectangular border. A powerful torrent of water erupts from the side of a rocky hill, gushing across the center of the frame. The scene includes stylized trees and distant mountain peaks under a pale sky.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

地生泉大水而兵亂

宋志曰

地無故自成泉天下亂兵起

大水

<vocab>朱文公, 宋志, 晉書, 陰晦, 陰謀, 兵亂</vocab>

<language>Classical Chinese</language>

<header>山鳴占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting in ink and color. It depicts mountains and valleys with a single figure in a green robe standing in the foreground, looking up towards the peaks as if listening to the mountains. The sky is a pale wash, and the terrain is rendered with soft brushwork.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

山鳴乃有大亂

宋志曰

山鳴乃有大亂

地鏡曰

山或晝夜有殼天下兵喪

范曄漢書曰

大山常鳴大如牛呐者天下
四分五裂之象

<column-break/>

<header>地拆裂占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting showing a desolate, cracked landscape. Broad fissures run through the earth, and sparse, leafless trees are scattered across the rocky ground. In the background, low hills rise under a grey, overcast sky, conveying a sense of geological upheaval.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

地拆裂而有聲者大臣失位天下不寧

宋志曰

秋行冬令則地裂其應士庶分離
大臣專恣下叛上賢者退不賢
者進裂而有聲四方不寧大兵
起國失厥土

晉書曰

地拆裂者臣下分離不相從也李
固以為陰類專恣將有分離之象

<vocab>朱文公, 宋志, 晉書, 地鏡, 范曄漢書, 占, 地拆裂, 山鳴, 陰類, 分離之象</vocab>

--- Page 13 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

<header>地燃占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting mountains and trees under a pale sky. Jagged red flames rise from various points on the ground and hillsides, suggesting the earth itself is burning.</image-desc>

朱文公曰地燃乃專恣自害之災

宋志曰
地燃者火為陽精地為陰主若或
燃則越陰之道行陽之政傷而不
剋之象臣專恣而終以自害也

天文錄曰
火者陽之精地者陰之主所以載於
萬物燃之者越陰之道行陽之政傷而
不剋之象非惟燃灼群生而自斃也陽者
君道陰者臣道臣下擅權終以自害也

<column-break/>

<header>天鼓占</header>

<image-desc>A celestial phenomenon is depicted in a dark, cloudy sky. A large, thick circular ring with four dark rectangular fittings (representing a "heavenly drum") floats above a landscape of hills and coniferous trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
天鼓乃有暴兵

宋志曰
無雲而雷名曰天鼓有暴兵
<gloss>作</gloss>

京房曰
天晝無雲而雷是謂天鼓所
當之國必有甲兵主國空虚

<vocab>地燃占, 天鼓占, 朱文公, 宋志, 天文錄, 京房, 陽精, 陰主, 暴兵, 擅權</vocab>

<header>天火占</header>

<image-desc>An illustration depicting "Heavenly Fire." Swirls of orange and red flames descend through dark grey smoke clouds onto the tiled roof of a traditional Chinese structure.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

天火乃虚偽淫靡之戒

宋志曰

天火災是火不炎上其信道不

篤而向虚偽修宫室無度則

厥災見而焚宗廟

晋書曰君高其臺天火其灾

<column-break/>

<header>地燃占</header>

<image-desc>An illustration depicting "Earthly Fire." Bright red and orange flames rise up from a dark grey, undulating ground surface into a smoky sky.</image-desc>

京房曰

火出地其國君殂

<vocab>天火, 地燃, 占, 朱文公, 宋志, 晋書, 京房, 災, 宗廟</vocab>

--- Page 15 ---
<language>Classical Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>
<header>地 湧 土 如 山 占 | 天 雨 土 霾 占</header>

<image-desc>A woodblock illustration on the left page showing a large dark mound of earth or stone rising abruptly from the ground, illustrating the omen "Earth Gushing Soil like Mountains." The image is framed within a rectangular border, with descriptive text below it.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
下人將起也地湧土如山
唐書曰
俞文俊上書曰天氣不和寒暑
隔人氣不和瘕贅生地氣不和
堆阜出今女主居陽位反陽則
柔故地氣隔塞山變為灾

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A woodblock illustration on the right page depicting several mountain peaks shrouded in a reddish-brown haze or falling dust, illustrating the omen "Sky Raining Soil and Haze." The sky is filled with a light wash of color to suggest the atmospheric phenomenon. Descriptive text is provided below the image within a frame.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
民勞而祿不肖則天雨以土霾
宋志曰
天雨土乃為霾曰上失其性而民
勞或不肖者祿
墨子曰
商紂不德十月土霾於亳
唐書曰
不肖者祿天雨土又曰百姓勞

<vocab>朱文公, 宋志, 墨子, 唐書, 俞文俊, 霾, 地湧土, 天雨土, 瘕贅, 堆阜, 商紂, 亳</vocab>

--- Page 16 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>
<header>天雨木冰占 地生乎光怪占</header>
<vocab>天雨木冰, 地生乎光怪, 朱文公, 宋志, 洪範傳, 漢書, 占卜, 災異</vocab>

<image-desc>A woodcut-style illustration with light ink and watercolor washes depicting a storm. Dark grey clouds fill the upper part of the frame. Heavy diagonal streaks of rain lash against three sparse trees, which bend to the left under the force of the wind. The branches of the trees appear thick and coated in a layer of white ice or frozen glaze.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

貴人將死也雨木冰如介

宋志曰

雨木雨冰大臣函陰氣附木故
木冰

洪範傳曰

冰一名介者兵之象也

班固漢書曰

天雨木冰后妃崩臣為亂

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A woodcut-style illustration with light ink and watercolor washes showing a dark, gloomy landscape. Under a heavy grey sky, a series of bright red and orange flames or glowing ethereal lights emerge from the surface of the earth, following the slope of a hill.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

賢滅而用小人則生乎光怪

宋志曰

地忽生光如火照國憂危亡
又小人進賢人滅也

--- Page 17 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>
<header>天雨電占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting depicts a dramatic storm. Swirling, dark grey ink washes in the upper right corner represent heavy clouds, from which long, diagonal, parallel brushstrokes in grey and blue descend across the scene, symbolizing torrential rain or lightning. Below, rounded, brownish hills are visible, with small, windswept trees clinging to the slopes, suggesting the power of the natural elements.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

雨電雨霽夷狄侵侮而臣后專

宋志曰

天雨電則陰負陽凡陽盛則為雨
若陰迫之則轉而為電蓋有所
脅也

班固漢書曰

電者陰脅陽為有所脇行專
一之政

<column-break/>

<header>山徒占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting illustrating mountain peaks in ink and light color. On the right, a large, craggy mountain massif rises with green-tinged peaks. To the left, a smaller, upright rock formation stands somewhat isolated on a flatter plane, with other smaller hills in the distance. This visual arrangement illustrates "山徒" (shifting mountains), a phenomenon regarded as a dire omen. Sparse, dark trees are scattered throughout the rocky terrain.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

山徒社稷亡

宋志曰

山或移徙是謂皇之不極祿去公
室賞罰出於私門天下兵<unclear>𩰚</unclear>起

尚書金滕曰

山徒者君不用道祿去賞罰
不由君私門成群名為皇之不極

<vocab>朱文公, 宋志, 班固, 漢書, 尚書, 社稷, 皇之不極, 占, 雨電, 山徒, 夷狄, 脅</vocab>

<header>山 崩 水 湧 占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese ink and wash painting depicting a natural disaster. On the left, powerful, stylized waves with white-capped crests suggest a flood or rushing water. On the right, a rocky mountainside with a gnarled pine tree stands against the turbulent water, illustrating the theme of mountain collapse leading to surging waters.</image-desc>

晉書曰

山陽君也水陰百姓也天戒若曰君道崩壞百姓將失其所故山崩或移徙故水湧

<column-break/>

<header>山 崩 占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese ink and wash painting showing a mountain landslide. Several large boulders are depicted falling from a jagged cliff on the left side of the frame. 朱文公曰

山崩而君道壞

宋志曰

山或崩毀者是謂陽盛陰弱崩者離散之象賢人去而佞人進

晉書曰

小人剥盧厥妖山崩兹謂陰盛陽弱勝強

<header>天無雲而雨占</header>

<image-desc>An illustration of jagged green and grey mountain peaks beneath a large, dark, swirling storm cloud. Inside the cloud, three barrel-shaped drums are linked vertically, a traditional symbolic depiction of thunder.</image-desc>

隋書曰
天無雲而雨者反臣伏誅也

## 日未入而無光占

<image-desc>A colored ink painting in the traditional Chinese style depicting mountain peaks under a grey sky. In the upper left, a red sun is encircled by a thick, dark greyish-black halo. 宋志曰

<header>星月晝見占；日闘則為雨乾占</header>

---
<image-desc>A landscape painting of jagged mountains and peaks in shades of green, blue, and brown. In the sky, a large red sun is partially eclipsed by a dark crescent moon. To the left of the sun, several golden star-like dots are visible in the daytime sky.</image-desc>
---

朱文公曰
星月晝見則為爭明小國
強而大國弱
宋志曰
日月與天並晝見是謂爭
明大國弱而小國強有王侯
日無光而星月有光者國危
主天下不安及天下不能正

<column-break/>

---
<image-desc>A landscape painting featuring rugged, craggy mountains and coniferous trees. The sky depicts two red solar discs positioned close together, with one appearing to have a faint, larger circular outline or halo. A smaller white circular orb is visible to the far right.</image-desc>
---

朱文公曰
日闘則為雨兢而為夷兵
宋志曰
日闘者別有假象共日體相
凌離而復合‥而復離日魄
失光乍明乍暗或鳥啼體俱
見在於食時之前晡晚以後
者為日闘雨侵之象天子惡
之

<header>妖日宵出占 飛流犯日占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting of dark, craggy mountains or islands set in water against a grey-blue sky. In the upper center, a single large red sun is encircled by a faint, diffuse halo.</image-desc>

大臣專政威權削奪
開元占曰三苗大亂天命殛之

宋志曰

日無光者民瘼死

<header>眾日並出占；當晝冥晦占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese ink and color landscape illustration within a double-line border. The sky features three red suns. 若兩軍相當數日並出有大
戰拔城當分營以應之

者為刑急人有不出一年有
大水晝昏而群鳥亂失分
土

<header>黑子若有黑色占</header>

<header>日中黑氣占</header>

### 黑氣如人在日中或如背臥占

<image-desc>An ink and color painting depicting an astronomical omen within a dark border. A solid red sun is positioned in the upper center of a pale sky. Within the sun's disc and floating around it are several dark, human-like silhouettes and amorphous black vapors. To the right of the sun, a small red triangular shape floats in the air. The bottom of the frame shows a landscape of jagged, stylized mountain peaks in gray and green.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
臣將叛則黑氣如人在日中
或如背卧
宋志曰
日中有雲如人或黑如人在
日傍者皆有叛臣

### 氣青如龍守日占

<image-desc>An ink and color painting depicting an astronomical omen within a dark border. A large, sinuous blue-green dragon with four legs and a long tail arches across the upper half of the frame, its head positioned near a solid red sun at the top center. The dragon appears to be "guarding" or encircling the sun. Below, a craggy mountain landscape is rendered in dark gray and black ink washes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
青如龍守日而臣謀
宋志曰
青如龍來守日臣有謀亦
戒飲膳扶日亦如之也
乾象新書曰
氣如青龍守日宜戒飲膳
下有謀

<vocab>朱文公, 宋志, 乾象新書, 叛臣, 龍, 占, 黑氣, 氣青</vocab>

<header>則日下雲如虎蹋占 則赤氣如輪在日側占</header>

<image-desc>A painting depicting a celestial omen. A large red sun sits in a pale sky. Immediately below it, a dark brown, shadowed cloud is shaped like a leaping tiger or quadruped moving toward the right. 朱文公曰

將叛則日下雲如虎蹋

雲如虎蹋在日中大將叛

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A painting depicting another celestial omen. The scene is set above a series of steep, jagged mountain peaks in green and grey tones.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

兵欲起則赤氣如輪在日側
亦似相扶

宋志曰

赤氣扶日或曲如輪在日傍
名曰扶皆為兵起失地

乾象新書曰 日傍氣如車
輪名曰提其下有兵亡地

**乾象新書曰**

### 如車馬走日下占

<image-desc>A traditional ink and wash painting depicting a celestial omen. A vibrant red sun hangs in the sky above a landscape of jagged mountain peaks, some with green moss, and dark pine trees. The style is characteristic of an illustrated manuscript on divination or meteorology.</image-desc>

如車馬走日下者軍破
宋志曰
氣漠漠如車馬馳走之状
在日下者有破軍一日有反
者披甲而走

### 若青馬青鳥在日下占

<image-desc>A traditional ink and wash painting illustrating an omen. A red sun is positioned in the upper right. The imagery corresponds to the accompanying text regarding portents involving "green horses" and "blue birds."</image-desc>

朱文公曰
若青馬青鳥向日下主有
憂慮
宋志曰
雲如青赤馬向在日下或兩
青鳥向在日下者人主有慮

日傍氣如蛇貫日則為兵起

<vocab>宋志, 貫日, 黑蛇, 白蛇, 兵起, 多雨</vocab>

--- Page 75 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

### 形曲背日為背占

<image-desc>An illustration of a red sun in a grey sky. Above the sun is a dark, ribbon-like cloud formation that curves away from the sun. Below is a landscape with dark rocky mountains and sparse vegetation.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
形曲背日為背為臣反為背逆
宋志曰
形曲向外則為背背叛也逆之象亦其下有叛者

<column-break/>

### 形曲向日為抱占

<image-desc>An illustration of a red sun in a grey sky. Beneath the sun is a yellow crescent-shaped cloud or atmospheric halo that curves around the bottom of the sun, as if embracing it. Below the sky is a landscape featuring steep grey cliffs and green pine trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
形曲向日者為抱為子喜而臣忠
宋志曰
氣黃曲向日為抱當有子孫喜臣下忠隣國來降
晉志曰
日旁如半環向日為抱
乙巳占曰
若兩軍相當則為和解也

<vocab>朱文公, 抱, 背, 占, 宋志, 晉志, 乙巳占, 氣黃, 背叛, 逆, 子孫喜, 臣下忠</vocab>

## 長而立者為直占

<image-desc>An ink and wash landscape painting depicting mountains and trees under a pale sky. In the sky, a red sun is shown with a single vertical red line positioned to its right, representing the "zhi" (straight) atmospheric phenomenon.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
長而立者為直
下有自立
宋志曰
長丈餘直立者旁為直氣則
下有自立者
乙巳占曰
其分有自立王者

<column-break/>

## 圓而小者為珥占

<image-desc>An ink and wash landscape painting of mountain peaks and sparse trees. In the sky, a red sun is flanked on both sides by small, curved red marks resembling parentheses, representing solar "ears" (parhelia).</image-desc>

朱文公曰
圓而小者為珥所臨者喜
宋志曰
氣圓小在日傍為珥所臨者喜
王朔曰
珥者耳也珥為近臣也又親近人
也當如耳也

<vocab>朱文公, 宋志, 乙巳占, 王朔, 珥, 直, 天文, 氣, 占</vocab>

<image-desc>A hand-colored landscape illustration framed in blue. It depicts steep, narrow mountain peaks with scattered pine trees. 朱文公曰

一珥為拜將而為攻戰

宋志曰

一珥者氣在日傍左者或右

者為喜兩軍相當欲和解

所在分無軍為禘祀

# 類雨直而相交占

<image-desc>An illustration of green mountain peaks under a pale sky. Above the mountains are three red celestial symbols: two large 'X' shapes flanking a central solid red circle. The characters "淫悖" are written in small black ink to the right of the mountain peaks within the illustration frame.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
類雨直而相交者為交淫悖内<gloss>亂</gloss>

宋志曰
狀類雨直相交淫悖内亂之象

乙巳占曰人主有淫悖之行則有此氣

開元占曰交者青赤如暈狀或如合背或正直交也

# 形如背而中起者為玦占

<image-desc>A landscape drawing featuring a small boat with a thatched roof on a body of water, positioned near rocky, vegetated hills. In the sky above the hills is a large solid red sun with a distinct red cloud formation resting on top of it; the cloud is horizontal with a raised, humped center.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
形如背而中起者為玦。傷戰北

宋志曰
形如背肢躰類山字則為玦見則君不和上下殃災

乙巳占曰
兩軍相當所臨者敗有軍必戰

<header>真橫於日上下曰格占</header>

<image-desc>A color landscape painting depicting a tall, blue-shaded mountain peak on the left with pine trees. In the center, above rolling hills, a solid red sun is positioned between two parallel, horizontal red lines.</image-desc>

<header>交曲於日左右古為紐占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting featuring a traditional building with a blue-tiled roof nestled among mountains and trees. In the sky, a red sun is flanked on both sides by vertical, gray-toned patterns resembling interlocking loops or braided "X" shapes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

交曲於日左右為紐，則為喜

宋志曰

氣小而圓在日下左右為紐氣

者善也

乾坤實曲曰

人君有納寵進幸之象也

<header>日上有戴氣占</header>

<image-desc>A colored ink painting of an atmospheric phenomenon involving the sun. 之

<header>氣小在日下而向上者為纓占</header>

<image-desc>A colored ink painting of an atmospheric phenomenon. 君有德令

朱文公曰

降

## 日上下有冠紐占

> 順虹而戰

# 日暈有抱珥占

<image-desc>A colored illustration depicting a complex solar phenomenon. The sun is represented by a central red disc surrounded by a thin green halo. It is flanked by two sets of symmetrical arcs: inner reddish arcs known as "bao" (hugging) and outer green arcs known as "er" (earrings/parhelia). To the left, additional multi-colored crescent-shaped symbols of atmospheric phenomena are visible. The landscape below consists of a windswept hill with sparse trees and several distinct, vertical rock pillars standing in the background.</image-desc>

其別有抱珥之屬尤主軍兵
之事

言之屬者非止抱之類

# 日暈在卯辰巳上占

<image-desc>A colored illustration of a solar halo. The sun, a solid red circle, is encircled by a wide, layered halo of blue and yellow-gold. The sky is light blue. To the right, two prominent, jagged rock peaks rise from a flat area.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

日暈卯辰巳方月內多雨

乙略例曰

月暈在卯辰巳言天子憂
民月內多雨

<header>抱 珥 在 暈 外 占</header>

<image-desc>A hand-colored woodblock illustration depicting an atmospheric phenomenon. At the center is a red sun surrounded by a multi-colored halo (green and red rings). Two pairs of reddish-brown curved arcs, identified as "bào ěr" (parhelia or sundogs), are positioned outside the main halo. Below the sky, the landscape consists of stylized rocky cliffs on the right with trees on top, and a lower rocky formation on the left near a body of water.</image-desc>

<header>日 暈 抱 珥 在 內 占</header>

<image-desc>A hand-colored woodblock illustration showing solar phenomena. A red sun is enclosed by a green and red halo. 抱珥在暈內圖城則城內勝

暈內有抱珥圖城者軍內人勝

<header>日暈有一抱一背占</header>

<header>日暈上下有負氣占 | 日暈四背在皆内占</header>

<image-desc>This illustration depicts a solar halo phenomenon with "backing auras" (fu qi). A solid red sun is centered within three concentric halos of blue-green, yellow, and red. <image-desc>This illustration depicts a solar halo with "four backs" (si bei). A solid red sun is surrounded by a single thin halo. 日暈四背在内名曰不和有内亂者

## 半 暈 有 一 背 一 玦 占

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese painting illustrating an atmospheric phenomenon over a landscape. 不成

# 日暈有背珥直虹貫占

<image-desc>A color manuscript illustration depicting a celestial omen involving the sun. A central red circular sun is enclosed by a thin white circular halo. On either side of the sun, positioned on the halo, are two inward-curving colorful arcs (parhelia, referred to as "ears"). Below the sky, a landscape of craggy mountains and trees is depicted in ink and light colors.</image-desc>

暈有背珥虹直兹順虹所
指攻

宋志曰
暈有背珥直而虹貫之或暈
而貫之至日或不至日順虹所
指從所擊之勝

# 負氣在暈上占

<image-desc>A color manuscript illustration of a solar phenomenon. A red sun sits at the center of a circular halo composed of concentric rings of red, yellow, and blue. Resting directly on the top edge of the halo is another curved multicolored arc, known as "carrying breath" (fuqi). The celestial event is shown above a landscape featuring a prominent steep cliff on the left and hills with trees on the right.</image-desc>

暈而負氣者暈上負為
喜亦為得地

開元占曰
赤青如半暈箸暈上為
喜亦為得地也

<header>月大而光占</header>

<image-desc>A colored landscape illustration showing a large, bright full moon in the sky. Inside the moon is the silhouette of a rabbit (the Jade Rabbit). The moon illuminates a night scene of a rocky hillside with sparse trees.</image-desc>

**朱文公曰**

月者闕也為陰主臣

**宋志曰**

月者太陰之精闕者盈極必缺也其體主夜為陰為后為臣為妻妾為水

**京房易傳曰**

天下和平上下俱昌則月大而光

<header>日蝕有兩珥四珥占</header>

日蝕有兩珥四珥而白雲中出者以日占事

**宋志曰**

日蝕有兩珥四珥而白雲中出甲子日天下有兵丙子日天下疫戊己日有兵喪庚申日下迫上壬癸日土工興

### 晦而月見西方占

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting shows a thin white crescent moon in a dark sky. Below, stylized mountains with blue and green washes are interspersed with pine trees. A prominent, rounded peak stands in the center-right of the composition.</image-desc>

---

朱文公曰
晦而月見於兌臣下驕盈
宋志曰
晦而月在西方是在日前為太
疾日朒人君舒緩臣下驕盈專
權之象
京房易傳曰
晦而月見西方謂之朓則侯王
舒緩劉向曰朓者疾也在君舒
緩在臣驕慢故日行遲月行疾謂
侯王專恣臣下侵急故月行疾也

<column-break/>

### 朔而月見東方占

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting features a full moon glowing with a yellow halo, containing the distinct silhouette of a rabbit (the Jade Rabbit). The landscape features a tall, narrow green mountain peak and rocky outcroppings with pine trees under a light sky.</image-desc>

---

朱文公曰
朔而月見於東人君必嚴肅
宋志曰
朔而見東是在日後為太遲日
朒人君嚴肅臣下危懼之象
京房易傳曰
朔而月東方謂之反反則侯王
嚴肅劉向以為反者不進之意
君肅急則臣恐懼故日以疾月
以遲不敢迫近君也侯王縮朒
不任事故月行遲也

<vocab>朔, 晦, 朓, 朒, 朱文公, 京房, 劉向, 宋志, 占卜, 天文</vocab>

## 月進退不常占

<image-desc>A colored landscape painting within a rectangular frame. On the upper left, a bright white moon hangs in a pale blue sky. The landscape below features stylized rocky mountains in shades of green and brown, with sparse pine trees. On the far right, a small white building with a dark tiled roof and red accents is nestled at the foot of a tall, pointed mountain peak.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
進退不常也臣后專政
宋志曰
臣及女后外戚專政則月多
失道遲疾進退不常故月
曰有變人主當賜赦寬刑恤
獄可解也

## 月行九道於中道占

<image-desc>A colored landscape painting within a rectangular frame. On the left side, a tall, craggy cliff with pine trees dominates the foreground. In the center, there are lower, rounded green hills. On the upper right, a bright white moon is positioned against a pale blue sky.</image-desc>

行陰道則陰雨行陽道則
旱風
宋志曰
明王在位則月行依九道月
上下皆明

<header>月行中道占 / 月有黃芒或戴氣占</header>

<image-desc>The right-hand page shows a white moon on a path marked by red circles (stars), set above a landscape with a pavilion, trees, and hills. The left-hand page depicts a celestial body (the moon) with four prominent rays and a blue crescent-shaped arc above it, signifying "wearing an aura." Below it is a landscape with sharp, colorful mountain peaks.</image-desc>

# 月行中道占

> 朱文公曰
>
> 行於中道者天下安寧

> 景佑占曰
>
> 天之三門猶房之四表中央曰天街天之中道天門也月順軌道由于天街則天下和平

> 河圖帝覽嬉曰
>
> 月行中道是謂安寧天下和平

> 京房曰
>
> 天下大安五穀豐登人主益壽則月行天子門也

<column-break/>

# 月有黃芒或戴氣占

> 朱文公曰
>
> 君后福昌黃芒或戴

> 宋志曰
>
> 君道福昌后妃有喜則月有黃芒或有戴氣

> 夏氏曰
>
> 黃芒
>
> 君有福昌后妃有喜月上有

> 荆州占曰
>
> 月有戴氣不出一百日人君有喜

# 月當望而不望占

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a rectangular frame. It features a prominent mountain with blue peaks, stylized pine trees on the slopes, and a waterfall flowing down a rocky cliff into a river. A bright, round full moon is positioned in the upper center of a sky with blue and grey washes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
當望而不望則主不昌攻人
地者有缺
宋志曰
月當望而不望者人主不昌
攻城不克
河圖帝覽嬉曰
月當望而不望者攻他人地
者有殃所在宿下國亡

# 月未當望而望占

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a rectangular frame. 月未當望而望則有更令

兵攻他人地者大吉昌

# 月當望内不見蟾蜍占

# 月中有氣如人行占

<image-desc>A painting depicting a full moon in a pale grey sky. Inside the moon is a dark silhouette of a person in a walking or running posture. Below the moon is a landscape featuring jagged, rocky mountain peaks and sparse trees.</image-desc>

若其中有如人行而相争則
客勝

<column-break/>

# 月下有雲氣如人相隨占

<image-desc>A painting showing a bright full moon in a blue sky. Below the moon, four dark human-like figures are depicted as if floating or walking in a line. The terrestrial landscape includes blue-toned mountains, a traditional building, and various trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
->氣如人随月所當之者侯王<-

宋志曰
月下有氣如人相随是謂惡
城其分侯王有憂

荆州曰
月下氣如人相随者是謂惡
城其分侯王有憂

<vocab>月, 氣, 占, 朱文公, 宋志, 荆州, 侯王, 客勝, 惡</vocab>

--- Page 147 ---
<language>Classical Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

<header>月旁赤雲如人頭 占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting a night scene. A pale, circular moon hangs in a dark sky. To its left are two reddish-brown clouds shaped like human heads in profile. Below, there are stylized mountains with green and blue pigments and several trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
雲若人頭在旁赤戰白兵黑雨
宋志曰
若日旁多雲如人頭占其色
赤為大戰白為多兵黑為多
雨一曰白多為風
河圖帝覽嬉曰
月旁亦雲如人頭其下大戰

<column-break/>

<header>月旁有白雲如杵 占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape illustration under a full moon. To the right of the moon is a bright white cloud shaped like a "chu" (杵 - a pestle or club), with its wider end pointing toward the moon. The landscape below shows steep mountain cliffs and dark trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
其旁如杵月將死兵亡
宋志曰
月下有氣如人相隨月旁白
雲大如杵抵月有戰軍破將死
河圖帝覽嬉曰
月旁雲白如杵有大戰破軍
將死

<vocab>占, 月, 雲, 朱文公, 宋志, 河圖帝覽嬉, 杵, 兵, 戰</vocab>

<header>赤雲來刺月占 黑雲來刺月占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese ink and wash landscape painting depicting a night scene. A full white moon is positioned in the upper sky, with a reddish-brown, elongated cloud appearing to "pierce" it from the right. Below the sky, there are rugged mountains, cliffs, and pine trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
雲氣或有來刺月赤戰
宋志曰
月旁有雲刺之赤敗軍相當
高宗占曰
赤雲刺月是謂仇賊

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting similar to the first, but with a different celestial omen. A white full moon is being "pierced" by a dark, jagged black cloud. The landscape below features mountains, a small bridge over a stream, and various trees.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
或有雲來刺月占黑為雨
宋志曰
黑色則為雨踰時也
高宗占曰
黑雲刺月陰雨踰時

<vocab>赤雲, 黑雲, 刺月, 占, 朱文公, 宋志, 高宗, 敗軍, 仇賊, 陰雨, 踰時</vocab>

害君長五穀不熟

<header>雲氣鳴鷄飛鳥群羊豕占 / 白雲刺來月占</header>

<image-desc>A rectangular framed illustration shows a central white moon surrounded by black silhouettes of various animals including a rooster, birds, sheep, and pigs in a circular arrangement. To the right is a vertical landscape of blue and green mountains with sparse pine trees and foliage.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
黑色如鳴鷄飛鳥群羊群
豕不雨則匈奴兵雨
開元占曰
有黑雲如群羊群豕飛鳥
鳴鷄在月旁如三五日不雨
匈奴兵起

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A rectangular framed landscape illustration featuring jagged green and blue mountain peaks with sparse trees. 朱文公曰
雲氣或有来刺白為喪
宋志曰
月傍有雲刺之色白則其下
軍亡
高宗占曰
白雲刺月其下軍亡

<header>月不暈而有珥占</header>

<image-desc>A hand-painted illustration of a celestial phenomenon. A bright full moon is positioned in the center-left sky, flanked on both sides by luminous, yellowish crescent-shaped arcs known as parselenae or "moon ears" (珥). Below the sky is a traditional landscape featuring dark, gnarled pine trees, jagged mountain peaks, and a small villa with a blue-tiled roof nestled on a hillside.</image-desc>

->朱文公曰<-

珥占其色青憂赤兵黃喜
白喪黑凶

->宋志曰<-

月不暈而珥入主有喜兵在
外有喜曰有珥占其色青憂
赤兵黃喜白喪黑雨又主國
凶兩珥十日內有雨也

<column-break/>

<header>一白 三蒼 二黑 貫月 占</header>

<image-desc>A hand-painted illustration of a night sky. A bright white moon is shown at the center-right, surrounded by dramatic, stylized cloud formations in white, dark blue (cyan), and black. The clouds are shaped like sharp, pointed streaks or wings that appear to pierce through the moon's disk. The lower half of the frame depicts a landscape with dark trees, rocky hills, and a small building in a valley.</image-desc>

->朱文公曰<-

雲生月側一白三蒼二黑貫
月則圍邑城降

->宋志曰<-

月旁有一白二蒼一黑雲其大如席
抵月或貫月圍邑則拔城

# 屠城氣占

<image-desc>A hand-colored illustration depicting military divination. Within a rectangular frame, a grey crenelated city wall is shown with a two-tiered gatehouse. A yellow banner featuring a red circle flies from a pole on the ramparts. Above the city, stylized clouds of red and dark brown vapor drift, representing the "qi" (aura) mentioned in the text. Below the illustration, several vertical columns of text provide interpretations of this omen.</image-desc>

> 朱文公曰
> 
> 或赤黑如狸皮

> 宋志曰
> 
> 或赤黑氣狀如狸皮斑其城
> 
> 破將敗

<vocab>屠城, 氣占, 朱文公, 宋志, 狸皮, 狸皮斑, 破將敗</vocab>

<header>屠城氣占</header>

<image-desc>A color illustration depicting a gray crenelated city wall with a two-story wooden watchtower on the left. 或雲氣如雄雉

宋志曰

雲氣如雄雉臨城其下必有降者

<header>屠城氣占</header>

<image-desc>A color illustration of a city wall featuring a gated entrance and a multi-story tower on the right. A yellow flag on a red pole stands near the wall. 赤如人頭飛鳥以敗軍

宋志曰

城上有雲如衆人頭赤色下
多流血又曰赤雲氣如飛鳥
如敗車城可攻急擊之破矣

### 屠城氣占

<image-desc>An illustration of a fortified grey stone city wall with a two-story red gatehouse on the right. A yellow flag with a red circular emblem is planted on the wall. In the dark grey clouds above the city, silhouettes of five standing human figures and several quadruped animals (resembling bulls) are depicted.</image-desc>

---

朱文公曰

或雲如立人五枚或如牛三邁
城圍或攻城

宋志曰

雲如立人五枚或三人在城上
其城可剋

<column-break/>

### 屠城氣占

<image-desc>An illustration of a fortified grey stone city wall with a two-story red gatehouse on the left. A yellow flag with a red circular emblem stands on the wall. Thick streaks of grey and yellow vapor emanate from the city and flow horizontally across the sky to the right.</image-desc>

---

朱文公曰

氣出向東或西而若此

宋志曰

其氣東出城可攻其氣西出
可降或氣北而出者為可剋

<vocab>屠城, 氣占, 朱文公, 宋志, 可剋</vocab>

<header>伏兵氣占</header>

<image-desc>A watercolor illustration showing a landscape with rocky hills and scattered polearms (spears or halberds) stuck into the ground, evoking a scene of hidden or ambushed troops. A prominent, elongated bright red cloud floats horizontally across the sky above the hills.</image-desc>

朱文公曰：
兩軍相當有赤氣隨氣所
在有伏兵

京房曰
兩軍相當有赤氣者伏兵之
氣若前有赤氣前有兵後有
赤氣後有伏兵左右亦同審
而察之即知伏兵所在也

<column-break/>

<header>屠城雲占</header>

朱文公曰
或有氣出而復入皆城客勝
之徵智將勿疑而急擊

宋志曰
其氣出城復入城中人欲逃
亡

必有伏兵

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A rectangular landscape painting framed in blue. It shows high mountains and green trees under a hazy, dark cloud. In the foreground and valleys, several swords and other bladed weapons are depicted partially obscured by the terrain, suggesting a hidden military force.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

或亦雲內之為人或數人之

在黑氣中其下伏兵潜伏

<vocab>伏兵, 氣, 占, 朱文公, 宋志, 幢節, 烏雲, 黑氣</vocab>

## 暴兵氣占

<image-desc>A landscape illustration showing green-tinted mountains and sparse trees. A horizontal band of vivid orange and red clouds stretches across the upper sky. Below, among the hills, several red military standards and a yellow ceremonial umbrella are visible, indicating the presence or movement of troops.</image-desc>

朱文公曰暴兵之象赤氣赫然
宋志曰四方清净獨有赤雲赫然
及壬子日獨見赤雲如旌旗其
下兵起若遍天下盡皆兵起
京房曰四方無雲獨有赤雲如
虹如狗如覆舟其下有流血兵
戰

<column-break/>

## 伏兵氣占

<image-desc>A landscape illustration depicting rugged, brownish-grey mountains. The sky is dominated by a heavy, dark, swirling mass of grey and black clouds resembling a storm or smoke. Several red spear-tips and banners are tucked behind the ridges of the mountains, illustrating the concept of "ambushed troops" hidden by the atmosphere.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
或雲如山嶽在外或前為後白
相隣此氣臨處伏兵藏伏而莫開
宋志曰
或雲如山嶽在外皆為伏兵之
氣

<header>暴 兵 氣 占; 孛 星 占</header>

<image-desc>
The page consists of two panels, each with an illustration at the top and text below.

The right panel shows a landscape with blue and green mountains under a red sky. Vertical, banner-like red streaks of light (qi) rise from behind the peaks. In the foreground, red and blue spears or military pennants are positioned among dark, scrubby trees.

朱文公曰

赤氣如旌旗四方遍滿兵起

宋志曰

又為賊兵暴起

<column-break/>

朱文公曰

光芒四出者曰孛，星為兵也

宋志曰

孛星光芒四出本類彗星小者
數寸長者横天體大無光假日
為光所指隨日辰見則東指
夕見則西指

晉書曰孛彗之屬偏指曰
彗芒氣四出曰孛又曰非當氣
之所生内不有大亂外必有大兵

# 格澤星占

<header>旬始近北斗状如雄雞伏鱉占</header>

<header>瑞星占 景星如月占</header>

<image-desc>The left side of the spread features a landscape with rolling hills in shades of green and brown. 景星如月而助月德厚合天

宋志曰

景星主德厚見則其國大昌

狀如半月生於晦朔助月為明

或曰星大而中空發號施令合
民心制禮作樂得天心也

晉書曰

景星如半月生於晦朔助月為
明亦名德星天子孝則海內歡悅德潤兮

<header>歸邪星占 / 天掊星占</header>

<image-desc>An illustration of a celestial phenomenon titled "Divination of the Guixie Star" (歸邪星占). The painting depicts a mountainous landscape with a large, glowing orange star at the center-right, surrounded by a circular, reddish-pink cloud or aura. The star and its aura are positioned above a craggy cliff with pine trees. Distant mountains are visible in light green and blue washes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
歸邪如雲而若星慶其歸國
宋志曰
歸邪如星非星若雲非雲或
生兩赤彗上向如張蓋而下有星
相連
漢書曰歸邪星如星非星如雲非雲
孟康曰歸邪星有兩赤彗上向如張
蓋狀下連星出必有歸國之慶

東出為掊而主奪争
晉書曰
天掊星本類彗星末鋭長三
四丈出東北主奪争

<header>司 危 星 占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting depicts a celestial event. In the upper left sky, there is a large white star with sixteen sharp rays radiating from a red center. 抱所以平非也

晉書曰

司危如太白有目出西方為
乖爭之兆主強擊見則失法

<header>天 衝 星 占</header>

晉書曰

見則臣謀主武舉卒發天子亡

# 六賊星占

<image-desc>A watercolor illustration of a celestial phenomenon. In the upper right sky, there is a red circular object with a long, wavy red tail tipped with a black point. <header>枉矢星如蛇行占</header>

<image-desc>A woodblock print illustration of a night landscape featuring jagged mountains, pine trees, and bamboo. In the sky, a celestial phenomenon titled "Wangshi" (Crooked Arrow) is depicted as a white circular star with eight black radiating points. It trails a long, black, wavy tail that undulates like a snake, extending toward the upper right.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
枉矢若流星如蛇行色蒼黑如有毛目及兵合射而所誅以亂伐亂而臣酷

枉矢方弩之象類大流星色蒼黑蛇行望之如有毛目長數丈

晉書曰
枉矢類流星色蒼黑蛇行望之如有毛目長數丈類匹布著天見則臣謀反兵亦為以亂伐亂

<header>天狗星流占</header>

<image-desc>A woodblock print illustration of a landscape featuring a three-story pagoda on the left, rolling hills, and various trees. In the upper right, a "Tiangou" (Heavenly Dog) star is shown as a red circle with a yellow center, streaking diagonally downward with a long, tapering, fiery orange and yellow tail.</image-desc>

宋志曰
天狗星流止地類狗聲所墜如火衡流血盈野伏屍滿谷白彗橫天斬強是主

宋志曰
天狗其星有尾旁有彗下如狗形又如奔星色黃有聲類狗

晉書曰
天狗聲黃有色其止地類狗所墜望之如大光炎衡天見則破軍殺將之兆

<vocab>天狗, 枉矢, 宋志, 晉書, 朱文公, 占, 流星, 彗星, 蒼黑</vocab>

<header>長庚星如布占</header>

<image-desc>A nighttime landscape painting under a full white moon. Across the dark sky, a long, thin, horizontal dark streak—representing the "Cloth" star—stretches across the sky. 長庚如一疋布着天見則兵起

<header>營頭星占</header>

<image-desc>A nighttime scene depicting a dark, dense, elongated mass—representing the "Camp-head" star—descending diagonally from the upper right toward a mountain range. To its left, a bright white moon is visible. The landscape below features a building nestled in a wooded hillside on the left and a series of rocky peaks to the right.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

營頭如壞山以墜軍大流如星
雷而晝出所墜有大戰而拔城
有覆軍流血而積骨

宋志曰

流星晝隕曰營頭有殼如雷
光照人野雉皆鳴群鳥皆驚

晉書曰

營頭有雲如壞山以墜所墜其
下有覆軍流血千里

<header>赤氣立見天占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting in ink and color. The scene depicts a small house or pavilion nestled among green-topped mountains and dark, leafy trees. In the sky above, several thick, diagonal red streaks represent a celestial omen known as 'red vapor' (chiqi).</image-desc>

朱文公曰

赤氣立見天極澤之氣伏屍之
象流血之徵

宋志曰

格澤氣赤如火炎上衝天上下
同色東西亘天南北可長四五
里為兵起其下伏屍流血

天鋒似鋒二星所見皆為兵起

天鋒彗象予鋒天下縦則天

<header>老子星占 | 燭星上有二彗占</header>

<image-desc>The left page of the manuscript spread contains three distinct framed sections. The top section is a narrow horizontal box containing the title "老子星占". The central section is a color illustration of a landscape with green and grey rocky hills, trees with dark foliage, and a large, bright white star in the sky representing the Laozi star (Canopus). The bottom section contains text in nine vertical columns of Classical Chinese script.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

老子則淳淳然色白者起大兵

宋志曰

色白淳淳然所見之地為飢為
凶為惡為怒又曰兵大起人主
憂除舊布新明大者為客星
與老子同占

晉書曰老子星淳白所見之國
為飢為凶

<column-break/>

<image-desc>The right page mirrors the layout of the left. The top box contains the title "燭星上有二彗占". The middle illustration shows a mountain landscape with a red celestial body (the Candle Star) at the top center, emitting two divergent white ray-like tails that point upwards, representing comets. The bottom section consists of eight vertical columns of Classical Chinese text.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

燭星上有二彗所見大盜不成

宋志曰

燭星入太白而不行不久而滅
或曰星上有二彗所燭地城邑亂
若出東方兵戰不勝出南方亡
地出西方其地有喪出北方色赤
其地兵勝

<vocab>老子星, 燭星, 彗, 占, 朱文公, 宋志, 晉書, 太白, 飢, 凶, 大盜, 客星, 城邑</vocab>

# 濛星如牙旗占

<image-desc>A rectangular landscape painting in traditional Chinese style. A prominent red celestial body, possibly a sun or a specific "red star," hangs in a pale sky. Below, sharp blue mountain peaks are depicted alongside several dark, vertical, pillar-like rock formations and green trees. The ground is a light brownish wash.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

濛星為亂也夜有赤色

宋志曰

濛星一曰刀星夜有赤氣如牙旗或長或短四面西南最多亂之象也或過天薄雲四方生赤黄氣長三尺乍見沒尋皆消滅見則兵起大戰血流

<column-break/>

# 蓬星楚楚然占

<image-desc>A rectangular landscape painting depicting a white comet with a long, bright tail streaking across a darkened sky. Below the celestial event is a landscape of mountains and trees, with a traditional Chinese gate or pavilion building featuring a dark tiled roof visible on the left. The scene uses ink and color washes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰蓬星楚楚然色青者穀聚

宋志曰蓬星大如二斗色白又曰色黃白方不過二三尺如夜火之光多則四五少則一二或出西南長數丈左右鋭出而見處其地受殃若出正東正南東北不旱即水五穀不成或如粉絮出比斗間為諸侯奪地

晋書曰蓬星大如二斗氣色白星見不出三年有亂臣

<vocab>濛星, 蓬星, 牙旗, 朱文公, 宋志, 晋書, 亂臣, 穀聚, 兵起</vocab>

<header>客星占</header>

<image-desc>A color manuscript illustration of a nighttime landscape. On the left side, a dark, weathered cliff rises, topped with pine trees and sparse vegetation. In the dark indigo sky, a solid black circular celestial body represents a "guest star" (客星). To the right of the cliff are two stylized green pointed hills and smaller shrubs growing from a rocky foreground.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
非其常有是為客星體小去遠
者事微而禍淺芒角見久者事大
而禍深黃為土功而得地赤為殺
將而侵城青黑則其下多病純
白則其分多兵

宋志曰
客星亦占其色青憂疾病赤而
芒角其下破軍殺將侵城奪邑
白色其分有兵喪黑則兵亡之
象皆以宿次分野占之

<column-break/>

<header>白星占</header>

<image-desc>A color manuscript illustration mirroring the previous page. On the right side, a rocky cliff with pine trees is depicted. In the sky, a bright white circular celestial body represents a "white star" (白星), likely a meteor or comet associated with mourning. To the left of the cliff are stylized green hills and sparse vegetation.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
白星為喪也似星非星

宋志曰
白星非星如星狀如削瓜主
有喪

<vocab>客星, 白星, 朱文公, 宋志, 占, 芒角, 分野, 兵喪</vocab>

<header>溫星出四隅占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a rectangular frame. It depicts stylized green and blue hills, sparse trees, and two large white circles in the sky, representing celestial omens. The style is characterized by delicate ink lines and light color washes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
溫星之出四隅所生如風動搖
而白色人飢大水而兵爭

宋志曰
溫星色白而大如風動搖常
出四隅東南則有兵將軍出于
野東北見暴兵千里西南見其
地兵喪並起西北見其地大水
人飢饉

<column-break/>

<header>蓬星狀如粉絮占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a rectangular frame, showing a building with a blue-tiled roof (likely a pavilion or temple) nestled among rocky hills and trees. A single large white circular celestial body is positioned in the sky above the landscape.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
蓬星狀如粉絮飢險或兵

宋志曰
星蓬狀如粉絮沸々主其下兵
飢或喪又為白衣會色青熒々
然所見之地風雨不時蝗熏
枯而物不生

晉書曰蓬絮星色青而熒々然
見則風雨不節五穀不登

<vocab>溫星, 蓬星, 朱文公, 宋志, 晉書, 占, 飢饉, 兵爭, 五穀不登</vocab>

# 飛 星 自 下 而 昇 占

<image-desc>A nocturnal watercolor landscape. A bright white orb, representing a "flying star," is shown rising from behind a dark, jagged cliff on the right, ascending into a deep indigo sky. The foreground features rounded hills and small trees, while distant blue mountain peaks are visible on the horizon.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
飛星自下而昇所之地曰有使
所墜之下有兵姦事乃蛇行
而曲：怒氣則較震而隆：
宋志曰
自下而上曰飛：至地曰墜
隆：者怒之象也
晉書曰飛星一名奔星亦流星
也自下而昇曰飛占與流星同

<column-break/>

# 流 星 自 上 而 降 占

<image-desc>A nocturnal watercolor landscape showing a bright white shooting star with two long, thin light trails streaking diagonally downwards from the upper right. The terrain below consists of steep rock formations on the left and green hills on the right with several small trees. The sky is dark with gray and blue washes.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
流星者天之使五星之散精也
乃飛行列宿占示休咎流星自上
而降自下而升皆有光景相連
晉書曰
流星天使也自上而降曰流出
則使者出

<header>雲氣入紫微垣占</header>

<image-desc>A watercolor illustration of a celestial omen. A multi-layered cloud of red, yellow, white, and black hues is positioned within an arc of red dots representing a star constellation (the Purple Forbidden Enclosure). 荆州占曰

有雲氣如雞雛出此子孫之
氣亦黃氣潤澤入天子有喜
黃白氣如旗起人主壽如杯碗
破入有壁白喜白氣出有喪黑
氣入有兵

<header>雲氣入文昌星占 | 雲氣入八穀星占</header>

文昌天之六府也黄雲氣入
三公受賜蒼白氣將相憂赤
黑氣入三公懸

<column-break/>

<image-desc>A landscape painting featuring green mountains and clusters of trees under a dark, ominous grey cloud. Enclosed within the cloud is the Bagu constellation, shown as eight small circles connected by lines.</image-desc>

<margin>天文錄曰</margin>

八穀主歳豐儉黑氣入萬物
不收大荒險

## 雲氣入東西掖門占

<image-desc>
A color landscape illustration within a rectangular border. ## 雲氣入太微垣占

<image-desc>
A color landscape illustration similar in style to the first. It features rocky, shaded mountains and green hills with pine trees. Two blue rectangular stone markers or towers are positioned in the valley. Above, a large white cloud contains a constellation of seven red dots. A streaming tail of yellow, red, and blue light extends from the cloud toward the right edge of the frame.
</image-desc>

天文錄曰
太微垣三公之庭白氣入有
光人主益壽黃氣如帚入有
婦人喜亦黃氣入天子有劔
喜氣如獸入天子用獸賜諸
侯白氣如抔椀入有壁玉喜
若有青黑赤氣入有憂

<vocab>雲氣, 太微垣, 東西掖門, 天文錄, 占, 端門, 三公, 諸侯</vocab>

# 雲氣入幸臣星占

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape illustration depicting trees with lush green foliage and a small pavilion nestled at the foot of rolling hills. In the sky above, a long, horizontally-oriented red cloud with a darker, denser core streaks across the frame.</image-desc>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting showing a shoreline with a small wooden boat moored near some bushes and a rocky outcrop. 晉書曰

尾宿為后妃之場后妃之府
蒼白氣入君有故臣来氣入
臣有亂

## 木星與金星合而交鬪占

<image-desc>A rectangular framed illustration depicting an astronomical omen. In a dark, hazy sky, two circular celestial bodies—one white and one dark green—are positioned close together. Below them is a landscape featuring stylized blue and grey craggy mountains and several trees with dark foliage and reddish-brown branches.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

合而交鬪兮軍破將死而內亂

宋志曰

木金合鬪國有內亂大將死野

有破軍又為水

<vocab>木星, 金星, 朱文公, 宋志, 占, 內亂, 破軍</vocab>

<header>木星土星合犯占</header>
<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a rectangular frame. In the sky on the upper right, there are two circular celestial bodies: a green sphere representing Jupiter (Wood Star) and a yellow sphere representing Saturn (Earth Star). Below them is a scene of misty mountains, a rocky cliff, and a small open pavilion structure.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
木土合犯有兵戰敵亦為謀
更代之事又為饑內亂之異
宋志曰
木土合野有兵相攻又曰土合
木有謀更代事上勢自弱又曰
戰必敗亦為飢
又曰為內亂民飢

土金合於太微國有大兵

# 金星與大星相守占

# 熒惑與辰星相合占

<image-desc>A landscape painting with jagged mountain peaks and dark trees. In the upper sky area, two celestial bodies are illustrated: one black circle representing Mercury and one red circle representing Mars, positioned side-by-side.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
若合則赤地千里
宋志曰
熒惑與辰星合為饑將出軍
行大敗有覆軍殺將在秋為兵
在冬為喪又曰為淬不可舉事
用兵及受其殃有軍不利
先起為主者勝入水故曰淬

合鬬於東外有兵戰合鬬於
西內有死王
宋志曰
木金合鬬有白衣之會

<column-break/>

<header>火星與土星合占</header>
<image-desc>A landscape painting with rolling mountains and scattered trees in ink wash style. In the sky, two celestial bodies are represented: a yellow orb on the left (Saturn) and a red orb on the right (Mars).</image-desc>
朱文公曰
熒惑合填為憂禍喪亦為大
人惡之又為舉事之殃
宋志曰
火與土合為憂又曰為禍喪其
國不可舉事用兵必受其殃又
為兵亂為旱為喪

<header>土星與水星合為壅阻占; 金星犯土星占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese watercolor illustration of a landscape featuring a small wooden bridge over a stream, bamboo groves on the right, and a rocky hill on the left. ### 朱文公曰
土水合處則為壅阻不可用兵卒事
或為更事變謀戰之客敗或有陰
謀

### 宋志曰
土與水合不可舉兵入兵得地出
兵無地又曰土與水合為壅阻水惟癰
而借土故曰壅阻主令不行所在
之分不可舉事用兵當受其殃
破軍殺將出兵亡地入兵得地若
賊敗一曰變謀更事為飢為早又
曰亡國失地有陰謀若合虛中
齊國地動秋水出

金犯土太子有憂

朱子曰

氣清白如膏則大戰將勇

天昏漠寒剋則遇敵相攻

宋志曰

初出軍日天氣昏漠雲氣陰
沉寒剋者必戰若清净温和
風塵不動者不見敵亦不戰

# 圖 謀 氣 占

<image-desc>A hand-painted illustration depicting a mountain landscape under a light sky. 白雲氣如群行或如群羊徘
徊結陣來者為他國人來謀
我視所往隨而擊之可得
郗明曰
白氣群行結陣來者為他
國人來圖我不可應視所往就
而擊之必可得

<header>軍 營 氣 占</header>

<image-desc>A woodblock illustration of a military camp in a mountain landscape. Banners of various colors (green, white, red) are planted among the rocks. 朱文公曰

兩軍相當各占其氣以高厚實
長直之類為勝以下薄虛短括
之類為北

宋志曰

凡兩軍相當軍上之氣高勝
下厚勝薄實勝虛長勝短澤勝
枯臨戰以我西賊東為例西厚東
薄西長東短西高東下西澤東枯
則我軍必勝矣反之則敗也

及叛軍自敗

# 軍營氣占

<image-desc>A rectangular hand-colored illustration depicting a military camp. Several red-tasseled spears and banners stand around a central camp structure with a thatched roof. A distinct, horizontal yellow cloud or "aura" floats directly above the camp. Mountains and sparse trees are visible in the background under a pale blue sky.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

氣安則軍安而治散則軍亂
而躓

宋志曰

軍上氣安則軍安氣不安則
軍不安氣南則軍南三方亦
如之氣散則軍散敗

<column-break/>

# 軍營雜氣占

<image-desc>A rectangular hand-colored illustration showing a military scene with soldiers and red banners. A heavy, dark charcoal-grey cloud descends from the upper right corner, partially obscuring the mountain landscape and hanging ominously over the camp and its personnel.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

對敵有雲来而其勢甚卑是
賊必大至而急起嚴備

宋志曰

對敵有雲来甚卑下其陰覆
人上掩溝蓋道是大賊必至
食不及飽急起嚴備

<header>軍 營 雜 氣 占</header>

<image-desc>An illustration of a military camp in a similar mountain landscape. A dark, grey-black horizontal cloud or vapor hangs over the entire scene. 獨行無黑氣則賊不可得也

<header>軍營雜氣占</header>

<image-desc>A detailed color illustration of a military encampment set against a backdrop of jagged, blue-tinted mountains. The camp features several structures, including a prominent building with a tiled roof and various red banners or pennants on poles. In the sky above the camp, a long, horizontal, greyish-white cloud formation stretches across, tapering at both ends.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

新出軍行師占雲逆可也而
順可擊

宋志曰

新出軍行師有雲從後來謂
如東行日雲從西來因隨而擊
之勝若赤雲從東來則我軍者
敵軍已行我軍當敗宜也兵固
守餘方倣此以類而推之

<column-break/>

<header>吉凶氣占</header>

<image-desc>A color illustration of a fortified city gate or palace structure with multi-tiered roofs and red walls, surrounded by trees and mountains. Above the fortification, a distinct red, crescent or boat-shaped cloud floats, with a thinner grey cloud formation appearing above it.</image-desc>

朱文公曰

五色氤氳方蕭索輪囷是謂
慶世太平之應

宋志曰

凡雲紫赤色及若烟非烟若雲
非雲郁、紛、蕭索輪囷是謂
慶亦曰景雲喜氣太平之應見
則國有慶又曰雲含五色潤澤和
緩見於城上為景雲非氣非烟
五色氤氳為慶 卿雲又五色
為慶雲三色為矞雲外赤内青
亦為矞太平之應

> 
> 憂

<vocab>吉凶氣占, 朱文公, 宋志, 青氣, 白雲氣, 喪, 陣, 憂</vocab>

<header>吉 凶 氣 占</header>

<image-desc>A landscape painting of mountains and pine trees. A large, dark grey cloud or "vapor" (qi) flows from the right side towards the center and left, partially obscuring the mountain peaks under a grey sky.</image-desc>

朱文公曰
黑氣如道則有赦
宋志曰
黑氣如大道一條至四五丈長
則不見頭尾東西不過三朔
大赦天下

<column-break/>

<header>吉 凶 氣 占</header>

朱文公曰
赤氣如血流
宋志曰
赤氣如血流又曰赤氣漫漫
血色者流血之象

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a rectangular frame. It depicts pine trees and jagged mountain peaks shrouded in grey and green mist. <header>吉 凶 氣 濛 霧 占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional ink-and-wash landscape painting in a rectangular frame. It features a mountain range with stylized green and grey peaks and several dark pine trees in the foreground. 朱文公曰

日月不見而在天者為濛氣
前後不睹而在地者為霧其造
化推與曰霧者日月也陽氣之洩
也陰氣蒸而為雲故濛日月而
為霧

宋志曰

凡在天為濛在地為霧日月不
見為濛前後不見為霧象土氣也

<header>虹霓占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese landscape painting within a blue rectangular frame. > 權輿曰虹霓者日月之氣陰雨
> 之氣過日之光則暈為虹霓今
> 以水噴日自側觀之則暈為虹

宋志曰
> 陰陽之氣和則為雨露怒則為
> 風雷散則為虹霓雄曰虹雌曰蜺
> 又曰變出鮮盛為虹暗薄為蜺
> 虹者攻也陽者攻陰也蜺者囓
> 也災虫傷害於物

若攻城有白虹自外飲城中水者

繞之若令從其缺處擊之勝

### 黃帝占曰
白虹繞城不匝從虹缺所在擊之
勝也其缺處賊乃徙其地而破走也

<language>Traditional Chinese</language>

### 風覺總占

-> 風占 <-

風氣無常從四方
五經通義曰陰陽氣散為風氣無根本先儒以風從西方四隅來
故謂之覺
王冰曰風者教之始天之使也所以發號施令
其政鳴条啓拆其化鼓舞飄揚
王冰曰風氣所生正當鳴条啓拆若風氣施化則飄揚敷布其為
變則木拔草拆也
又曰景霽山昏蒼埃際合岸吞若一岩岫之風也黃白昏埃繞空

<column-break/>

<header>風 占</header>

<image-desc>A traditional Chinese ink and color landscape painting contained within a rectangular double-lined border. The scene illustrates the movement of wind. On the left, gnarled pine trees grow from rocky slopes, their needles slightly agitated. On the right, a thicket of tall reeds or bamboo stalks is shown bending sharply to the right, indicating a strong breeze. In the far background, light blue mountain peaks emerge from the mist. Above the top edge of the border, the characters "風" (right) and "占" (left) are written.</image-desc>

-> 風占 <-

風噫氣也生於四時起於八方其
時宜而風順則吉否則凶弟其
氣有聲無形不可圖像故但
列其占于左云

<vocab>風覺, 風占, 王冰, 五經通義, 鳴条, 啓拆, 敷布, 岩岫</vocab>

--- Page 292 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

勢紛錯交横任小人而疎君子
宋志曰風来其勢紛錯交横乍起乍止深藏難測其声貼耳者為
小人昧惑之風君任小人疎君子則至也
穀啾唧惨切不疾疢而必大喪
宋志曰風来其穀啾唧令人惨切為大喪之風
黄帝占曰或主疾疫也
憾、蓬勃大兵将至
宋志曰風来其勢憾、南北雜起高下蓬勃為大兵将至之風
災、恍惚大旱為災
宋志曰風若炎、如左奔馳乍起乍止為火旱之風

<column-break/>

如墻獨見天垂川澤之風也加以黄黑白埃齊下山澤之猛虎也
頌平則清和明静政治則天氣温凉
宋志曰凡風之類清和明静天氣温凉塵埃不起人民悦暢為祥
風祥風者端風也亦曰景風又曰和風人君政頌和平則祥風至
君聖政治則春無凄風夏無苦雨
尚書洪範曰聖時風若  董仲舒曰太平之世風不鳴條開甲拆萌
發屋拆木者為怒
宋志曰發屋拆木名曰怒風也
揚沙轉石者為狂
宋志曰揚沙轉石者名曰狂風也

<vocab>宋志, 祥風, 怒風, 狂風, 尚書洪範, 董仲舒, 黄帝占, 疢, 蓬勃, 啾唧</vocab>

--- Page 293 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

黃帝占曰風氣恍惚爽、盪人如大戎運或疾為大旱風也
大風黃霧方白日沉、主上昏亂政化未明
宋志曰風來其勢冥：白日沉、黃霧充塞大門翳精主上昏亂
政化未明　黃帝占曰主上昏亂政化未明
触塵蓬者為勃亂
宋志融塵蓬勃為勃亂之風也
扶搖羊角者為飄風
宋志曰扶搖羊角為飄蕩之風也
凜冽而人<gloss>憤</gloss>戰慄刑罰暴急
宋志曰風氣凜冽為刑罰暴急之風也

<column-break/>

卒起而南北不定上下不寧
宋志曰風來其勢暴急南北不定離合紛埃此為上下不寧之風
->八風<-
聖人在上時風乃若賢人在朝八風循道
宋志曰八卦各有王卦於本卦方上來者為王風從其中慶者為
逆風以時至則陰陽和化道成是為八風循道聖人在朝則其風
應時以為瑞慶王者當順八風而行八政　尚書洪範曰聖時若
立春有條風而艮生
宋志曰立春之節東北方艮卦用事赦小罪出桎梏則其卦氣應
而條風生如從冲來則為廷風主兵起其日若天氣晴明少雲則

<vocab>黃帝占, 宋志, 八風, 條風, 艮卦, 飄風, 勃亂, 桎梏</vocab>

--- Page 294 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

歲善而收阴則旱蟲傷禾稻
春秋考異郵曰條風者條達也連生也言此方上風來生萬物也
其日若風從
乾来暴雷殺物 坎来大寒北夷侵掠 艮来歲豊五谷收成
震来氣泄百物不成 巽来歳内多風百虫死傷 離来旱傷生物
坤来春多寒六月大小多愁嘆 兑来兵起霜早為災
春分有明庶而東作
宋志曰春分之節東方震卦用事正宜修田疇則其卦氣應而
明庶風至如從冲来則為逆風王者宜防左右若刑殺昏惨者凶
其日東方有青雲則歲必熟若清明則物不成

<column-break/>

春秋考異郵曰明庶風者明萌也庶衆也言此風衆物生也其日
若風從 乾来歳多暴寒金鉄暴貴 坎来飢疫殺豆不成 艮来
夏谷不熟米貴一倍 震来五谷成而歳康 巽来時生百虫四
月暴寒 離来五月先水後旱 坤来歳有小水人多癯疾 兑来
蝗虫大動
清明出巽當立夏之時
宋志曰立夏之節東南巽卦用事出帑帛使諸侯檢重囚出軽
撃断薄刑决小罪則其卦氣應而清明風至如從冲来則為逆風
主小凶其日南方赤雲生則歲豊若晴明則旱
春秋考異郵曰清明風者清精芒剗鋭時麦之屆秀出已備故其

<vocab>春秋考異郵, 宋志, 條風, 明庶風, 清明風, 震卦, 巽卦, 占候, 農業氣象</vocab>

鋒芒收割使成實也 其日若風從 乾来霜殺夏麥其年民飢
坎来其歳多雨魚行人道 艮来山崩地動人疫疾 震来雷動
非時震擊萬物 巽来其年谷豊 離来夏旱禾焦 坤来人多
不安萬物損傷 兌来蝗虫大動

### 景風南来入夏至之日

　宋志曰夏至之日 正南方離卦用事爵有功禄有德貴俊傑過
賢良則其卦氣應而景風至如徒冲来則為逞風臣子為乱其日南
方有赤雲則歳豊晴明旱
春秋考異郵曰景風者景強也強以成之言萬物之強盛也其日
若風從 乾来寒傷萬物 坎来四時不節憂日多寒 艮来山

<column-break/>

水漫出 震来八月人多疾患 巽来九月風傷中物 離来五
谷成熟 坤来六月雨水横流 兌来秋月多雨

### 立秋号西南凉風乍凉

　宋志曰立秋之節西南方坤卦用事専任有功以伐不義詰誅暴
慢以明好惡則其卦氣應而凉風至如徒冲来則為逆風谷無實
其日有白雲及雨 晴明清明則萬物不収
春秋考異郵曰凉風者薄寒以閉收也言明氣收藏萬物也其日
風從 乾来苦寒多雨 坎来多露多霜寒 艮来秋雨不和
震来秋多雨雹 巽来兵卒内起 離来 兵不利 坤来五谷
成熟 兌来主歳豊稔

<vocab>宋志, 春秋考異郵, 占候, 景風, 凉風, 夏至, 立秋, 八卦, 乾, 坤, 震, 巽, 坎, 離, 艮, 兌</vocab>

<columns>2</columns>

室脩園圃則其卦氣應不周風至如冲来為逆人去其郷其日晴
明小寒人君吉天下咸熈
春秋考異郵曰不周風者不交也陰陽未合化也 其日風若從
乾来王道行 坎来冬雪殺畜 艮来冬温夏早 震来冬温有
雷人疫 巽来蟄虫出户雷電作 離来五月大疫皆在来年
坤来水虫食物魚塩倍多 兌来山澤有兵人多妖言

### 冬至坎来方廣莫
宋志曰冬至之節正北方坎卦用事誅有罪斷大刑去声色禁嗜
欲安形性休甲兵不具禀以待陰陽之定則其卦氣應廣莫風主
如冲来逆人民散其日有雨雪寒則歲豊晴明則物不成

<column-break/>

### 秋分方西方閶闔欲剥
宋志曰秋分之節正西兌卦用事嚴刑罰飾囹圄誅殺必當罪無
冤滯則其日卦氣應閶闔風至如冲来為逆風凶其日西方有白
雲即歲豊晴明則物不成
春秋考異郵曰閶闔者當寒也天地收成閶闔盛時盛物盖藏之
意閶闔為當寒也 其日風從乾来人多相掠 坎来多水寒
艮来十二月多霜雪 震来大疫木華再荣不實 巽来十月多風
離来兵動國南 坤来土功大作 兌来主歲豊稔

### 立冬乾来方不周
宋志曰立冬之節西北方乾卦用事恤孤寡察阿黨築城郭造宮

<vocab>秋分, 立冬, 冬至, 閶闔, 不周, 廣莫, 兌卦, 乾卦, 坎卦, 宋志, 春秋考異郵, 阿黨, 妖言</vocab>

天官曰大水 巽来民疫 天官曰民相疾歳惡 離来大旱
天官曰文同 坤来小旱 天官曰文同 兑来无灾 天官曰兵
黃帝占曰歳且天氣清和溫潤風不鳴條若有雲逆迎日其歳美
無疾疫 萌曰正月朔日暮候東井上雲 有歳海務耕山田
高處又曰正月朔日曉至三日無風雨閉不見者一歳大美十倍
沛公占曰正朔日大風發屋拆木揚沙動石其歳大惡
黃帝占曰十二月晦正月朔風雨春旱

### 五音風占

何以別風之五音宮則如牛鳴嘷中急惡土功大興宫来山摧岸崩
宋志曰宫風屬土君子之象以宫日有風音如雷聲鼓聲如牛

<column-break/>

春秋考異郵曰廣莫言廣大莫測為上通無莫之風冬無見者
春秋感精符曰澡侯冬至之日見雲逆迎日者歳美人民和
不疾疫無雲逆迎日者歳惡赤旱白雲兵雲土功黒雲水宋
均占曰其日風從 乾来強國憂年多寒 坎来歳豊民樂 艮
来正月多震 震来時雷大發大雨連行 離来多疾氣婦多
亡水旱不時人多病 巽来虫傷生 坤来虫蝗傷稼多水
兑来秋雨大兵起
正朔之風立春同
乾来小雨兵起 天官曰戎菽胡豆也為戎也 坎来大雨滂歳
天官曰為上歳 艮来歳稔常昭曰歳穣 震来大水寒霖

<vocab>春秋考異郵, 春秋感精符, 五音風占, 天官, 占候, 八卦, 乾, 坎, 艮, 震, 巽, 離, 坤, 兑</vocab>

<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

商則如離群羊風聲入商暴起有關兵急令商來必責塞關梁
宋志曰商風屬金臣之象占以商日有風如離群羊如鐘聲一如
飛鳥之集羽發屋拆木主官宅自相傷及有關兵急令至關梁不
通其日有風從商來白衣聚関渠塞大將退外敵入
發宮方邑有憂
注云商日風從宮來國有憂將在外兵不戰
起角地國有表
注云商日風從角來國有表兵主行
君令行也生於徵上
注云商日風從徵來若君令行兵主凶

<column-break/>

嗚呼中發屋折木者主土功動君心不妥當有急令上下相疑來
年兵起其日風從宮來山摧岸崩人君出行泉出地裂
自角而來蝗虫散谷
注云宮日角位風從角上來蝗虫敗谷
從商而至大水暴風
注云宮日風從商來水泉湧暴風雨
發徵方虫狼為害
注云宮日風從徵來虫狼為害土功動
起羽地大雨寒陰
注云宮日風從羽來大雨寒陰大臣凶

<vocab>宮, 商, 角, 徵, 羽, 風, 占, 兵, 宋志, 蝗虫, 關梁</vocab>

### 大雨至也來於羽方
<gloss>注云商日風從羽來大雨至兵在外解</gloss>
<gloss>千人呼嘯其風聲配角振木則賊來戰聞</gloss>

發屋折木者懼大首兵起関梁不通城邑固守
其日風從羽來主雨雹霜灾
從商則兵関將憂
<margin>注云羽日風從商來有関兵不安邊將憂</margin>
日宮則暴寒傷物
<margin>注云羽日風從宮來主暴風大雨寒氣傷物</margin>
徵來兮臣民有憂
<margin>注云羽日風從徵來臣民有憂</margin>
角來兮城圖不去
<margin>注云羽日風從角來主城圖不解</margin>

角則旱大發而土功
<margin>注云徵日風從角來有土功起火灾時有火旱</margin>
宮則寺舍灾而哭泣
<margin>注云徵日風從宮來主寺舍灾有火焰哭泣</margin>
商為急兵聞爭
<margin>注云徵日風從商來有聞兵國不安邊將憂</margin>
羽為寶物出國
<margin>注云徵日風從羽來宝物出國多雷震有使命</margin>
揚波擊濕鼓孰不謂之羽急怒則雚首而有兵發羽則霜雹而大雨
宋志曰羽風属水物之象占曰羽日有風如擊濕鼓如水揚波聲

上來家人相牽引占則出行為禽獸所傷也

### 寅午為廉貞廷進專精

宋志曰南方之情惡火也惡行肅自寅午主之火生在寅盛於午惟灾猛無所容受故為惡氣精專而嚴正故曰廉貞其日風從本方上來相傍惡事興風從寛太上來時加巳酉為賔客以酒食燕樂迁官詔命至風從姦邪上來妖人為怪婦凌夫風從公正方上來貴人有事相問風從貪狠上來有人競財風從陰賊上來有賊無所尅也

### 亥卯酉為陰賊潜為盗寇

宋志曰東方之情怒木也怒行陰賊亥卯酉主之生於亥盛於卯

<column-break/>

# 六情風占

### 五音既定参之六情

古注云二者必参而用之

### 子為貪狠貪而無顔

宋志曰北方之情好水也好行貪狠申子曰主之水生於申盛於子其性馳物而潤多暗好好則貪而無顔故謂貪狠其日風從本方來不出七日間渠驚否則自兵攻他界若時加貪狠有賊却害人禍起北方時加姦邪風從陰賊上來有盗傷事風從公正上來有拆怨揚兵日擊年立忍凶風從姦邪上來惠人持物風從廉貞上來有人相賂風從寛太上來持禮相接而求人財物風從陰賊

<vocab>六情風占, 貪狠, 廉貞, 陰賊, 宋志, 五音, 申子, 寅午, 亥卯酉</vocab>

--- Page 302 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

其性受水而生其地而出為想為陰多則害土故為陰賊其日風
從本方上來不出七日有陰賊入界偷營寨風從姦邪上來時加
申子而止於申子上有賊盜風從貪狠上來白刃相傷風從公正
上來外人謀內風從寛大上來賈客施財與人風從庶貞上來有
巢穴之士為人所却

<margin>己酉為寛大酒食寬榮</margin>
宋志曰西方之情喜金喜行寛大己酉主之生於巳盛於酉金之
為物喜以利刃加萬物故喜為利也刃之所加無不寛大故為寛大
其日風從本方上來有酒食歡榮喜慶事風從庶貞上來時加
己酉為眷慶加賞賜風從公正上來有酒訟與風從陰賊上來有酒

<column-break/>

<margin>戊丑公正芳悲哀而報仇諫争</margin>
食相害風從姦邪上來因酒婦人口舌風從貪狠上來酒間争財
宋志曰下方之情哀也哀行公正戊丑主之下方南与西也陰氣
所谓生故為下方戌皆火也丑皆金也金剛火强各歸其郷火刑
於午金刑于酉皆其盛也則受刑至窮無所歸故曰哀火無私而
金剛故公正其日風從本方上來兩軍相當大将奉降不出七日
他兵敗時加公正吏人相爭仇人報怨風從奸邪上來時加庶貞
正人誹告部吏私物風從陰賊上來主下凌上風從貪狠上來離
失農心風從庶貞上來君長欲迂風從寛大上來出逢酒食
哀未奸邪也淫渋而誑詐虚驚

<vocab>宋志, 己酉, 戊丑, 陰賊, 寛大, 公正, 風角, 占卜, 五行</vocab>

<columns>2</columns>

衝情壬癸為鈞情伯支後三辰月支前三辰俱為鈞名假令申刑<gloss>己酉刑子是也</gloss> 京房曰六情者好惡喜怒哀樂也好行貪狼主欺詐不實亡財遇盜求人物強取事惡行廉貞賓主客礼儀嫁娶圖謀為人誠信主迁官事喜行寬大主爵禄賞賜聚積酒食慶賀事怒行陰賊主戰鬪殺傷謀反叛逆主劫暴事哀行公正主報仇諫諍事樂行奸邪主淫秩疾疫欺詒事

### 日辰大風

### 風塵蔽天干支同

<gloss>注云干為日支為辰也</gloss>

### 甲日則海中兵起

<column-break/>

宋志曰上方之情樂也樂行奸邪辰未主之上方北与東也陰氣所萌生故為上方辰亥水也未亥木也木落歸本流水歸源故木利在未水立在辰水穹無隙不入木穿則旁行故為奸邪其日風從本方上來七日內必有賊来及陰人誑詐虛驚風從寬大上來時加辰未有人持酒礼来相疾謀殺日時皆和奸風從公上來有文書詔命盜賊相牽風從貪狼上來賊人物逃風從廉貞上來因酒食為病風從陰殺上來宿人相攻

甲乙為本情而不動丙丁合戊己則參刑衝是庚辛壬癸取諸情而自辨陰前陽后合三辰是日鈞名

風角占曰六情者甲乙為本情丙丁為合情戊己為刑情庚辛為

<vocab>宋志, 風角占, 六情, 京房, 鈞名, 貪狼, 廉貞, 廉貞, 日辰大風, 庚辛, 壬癸</vocab>

--- Page 304 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

宋志曰甲日大風海中兵起
乾坤寶典曰謂甲日大風必以丙丁日雨不雨則海中兵起
乙日夷狄來侵
宋志曰乙日大風作粟貴夷狄侵邊
丙丁日旱疫而邊兵圍城
宋志曰丙日大風邊兵圖急丁日大風旱疫
戊己糴貴而土工色迁
宋志曰戊己日大風土工與食物貴或迁邑
庚辛日呼怒急脩邊強無咎
宋志曰庚辛日若風声叫怒者宜脩邊

<column-break/>

壬癸飛沙北虜侵境不寧
宋志曰壬癸日若風飛沙拆木者北虜侵邊
已上三日內有雨即解陽怒利以陰承
京房曰風為陽雨為陰陽怒而得陰故解
子為兵起水中
宋志曰子日大風日光沉沉兵起水中
丑為粟貴之徵
宋志曰丑日大風掃地揚沙者粟暴貴
寅日有赤氣在災天
宋志曰寅日大風而赤氣四塞者有火災

<vocab>宋志, 乾坤寶典, 京房, 占卜, 風角, 兵起, 夷狄, 糴貴, 脩邊, 庚辛, 壬癸, 甲乙, 丙丁, 子丑寅</vocab>

--- Page 305 ---
<language>Classical Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

卯日有黃霧則虫生
宋志曰卯日大風而黃塵冲天蝗虫必生
辰為將行
宋志曰辰日大風大將出行
巳為天下大旱
宋志曰巳日大風若蓬勃無常者有大旱
午為民散
宋志曰午日若風乍疾乍遲者人民不安流離散亡也
未為土工大興
宋志曰未日大風日無光彩者土功大興也

<column-break/>

申則盗攻粟倉
宋志曰申日風聲如磬者賈盗攻
酉作河瀆漲溢
宋志曰酉日風肅々濡潤必大水河瀆橫流
戌則胡兵四起
宋志曰戌日大風塵霧橫天揚沙千里胡兵四起
亥為兵盗相攻
宋志曰亥日大風揚沙折木者兵賊相攻人民不安三日有雨即
解陽怒利以陰象 京房註同
察兵勝負風占

<vocab>宋志, 卯, 辰, 巳, 午, 未, 申, 酉, 戌, 亥, 蝗虫, 河瀆, 京房, 風占</vocab>

--- Page 306 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

風從子午宮來者主人大敗亦為攻城得捷邊城被陷他皆傚此推
運風雨交戰則師從大敗而名落屍

> 宋志曰初出軍及三日內行兵風勢蓬勃逆來沖我旗幟難舉
人聲怯馬不斷吹沙馳塵人馬經過步迴無跡者此名鬼風或出
軍三日及交戰有急風其雨濡濕軍馬咸不能振者此為軍敗若
天氣昏風聲錯亂或久陰不雨皆為下有人謀宜設謀益以順
天意又曰出軍而飄風驟來牙旗摧折旗幟繞竿或下垂者交
戰將死若營陣既成而有暴風卒來相掩軍幕傾旗幟折木者
為惡逃主將失兵叛散 風覺占曰初出軍無風草木不動者賊
不可得人曰若軍行旗幟指向前者三軍大敗將死若半道逢賊分

<column-break/>

兩軍相過風占勝負先明其日納音次占起時方所其日納音為客
時與來方為主

> 宋志曰兩軍相當未明勝負以所占時與來方為主其日納音為客
時方制音利為主而后應納音及制宜為客而先舉返若相生是為
和睦

> 宋志曰若時與來方能制納音者主勝及之客勝以相生為比和
不戰 子午為宮音 丑未寅申為徵音 卯酉為羽音
辰戌為商音 巳亥為角音

> 乾坤寶典曰假令壬子癸丑木壬午癸未木戊辰己巳木皆為角
日為客風從辰戌角上來時加辰戌者主人勝客軍敗若此角日

<vocab>兩軍, 占, 勝負, 納音, 宋志, 乾坤寶典, 風覺占, 旗幟, 宮商角徵羽</vocab>

--- Page 307 ---
<language>Classical Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

天氣昏濁風勢寒烈塵土蓬勃者即不利若入敵境径要害過城
壘三日內有風雨雷霆從我軍上去為龍虎相助即急戰必大勝
若安平之時風雲相交即雨至用兵之時龍虎相助即急戰風為
主雲為客若風勢緊大雲氣薄少者主勝若雲色濃厚来急風勢
微弱不能震怒者客勝
風角占曰假令角日風雲從辰酉上来此名母詞子風從丑未寅申
上来此為子扶母出軍當勝有成功
神武秘畧曰風為龍為主人雲為虎為客人
受宮羽商日風從季来或申子亥卯時當子午刑殺急速而寒濁
有羗兵犯塞而出沒

<column-break/>

軍雨向或戰或止風亦逆吹旗幡却飛不進者大敗　又曰若風
雨逆來不沾衣者此名天泣軍師戰大敗若交戰風雨兼来者此
名落屍戰必大敗
神武秘畧云若狂風吹足昂起空中者其軍不利宜安撫軍旅及
祀天地吉又曰若旋風入寨大為不利
順風雨交戰則軍旅大捷而為得助
宋志曰初出軍日風從后来申　从出人雄壯馬嘶逸旗如舉
勢指敵方鼓角清而響者全勝從南方来而前向者得天人之助
獲敵粮儲敵人来降若風從地上五音相生来生我為母詞子我
生為子扶母也天色晴明人馬奔逸上下歡心風順和暢者軍勝

<vocab>神武秘畧, 宋志, 風角占, 龍虎相助, 母詞子, 子扶母, 天泣, 落屍戰, 五音</vocab>

<language>Chinese</language>

<columns>2</columns>

### 右頁

宋志曰當視四季受宮之日風從四季上來飛土揚塵發屋拆木
而天色温和時加子午五音及子午止者當有邊夷入貢寒則
不至受商受羽受宮之日風從四季上來其勢叫怒當子午兼帶
刑殺或申子亥卯上來其勢急速者當有夷兵犯塞風勢寒尅
必成也

->受角日子午之時季來則將戎夷去<-

宋志曰四季受角之日時加子午風勢滕急侵四季上來中國將
伐四夷風止之夷為所伐之地止辰伐東夷止未伐蠻夷止戌伐
西夷止丑伐北夷 若風止有雨景色温和即不行

->受角日徵赤則火灾<-

---

<column-break/>

### 左頁

宋志曰受角之日大風發屋拆木從丑寅未申徵方上來乍速乍
遅時加子午火當發宮寺

->受羽日羽來則暴雨至<-

宋志曰受羽之日風從卯酉方上來天陰雲潤人心悲惨連三日
者當有暴雨大水驟至又有風雨肅々調和潤氣濡物從卯
酉之位來必酒更而雨至

->自刑日風起徵方大火起灾及貴府<-

宋志曰辰午酉亥自刑之日風從徵方來者有火灾風急迅寒
者火殺人三日内有雨即解

<margin>飄驟而牙旗折交戰將死</margin>

---

### 入敵城急宜攻擊

旋入敵城宜從所入亟攻之
入宮宅屋室之內映音情刑德為先
宋志曰旋風入者當以五音六情占之用旺相休囚刑德以分憂
喜從德位來為吉事刑位為憂事

### 德為喜慶

從歲月日德方上來為喜慶吉事

### 刑為憂煩

從歲月日刑方上來為憂惱凶事
入屋室飛揚衣物驚財貨不盜須愁

<column-break/>

注云出軍而瓢風驟來牙旗摧折旗旛繞竿或下垂者交戰將死
急急而軍幕傾將卒皆患
注云若營既成而暴風來相撩軍幕傾旌旗折林木者為
惡主將失位兵叛散
暴風急起於刑墓之方宜防急賊及伏兵掩覆
注云若有疾風於歲月日刑上來者有急賊至或前後有伏兵
掩人不備宜謹防之

->旋風占<-

融塵盤桓風名曰旋入吾寨急宜嚴備
旋風入吾寨大為不祥亦為狂賊來嚴備之吉

<vocab>旋風占, 牙旗, 刑德, 五音, 六情, 旺相休囚, 瓢風, 融塵</vocab>

## 行軍風雨

軍行發時有風雨者當破敵大勝此謂得天道風若隨軍順不計冲
方位皆吉有風無雨 歌曰
> 兵戈整理款前征 無雨生風便可行
> 必獲敵人城邑地 急須惟此取功名

行軍及屯營風動旌籓不動如無風一狀兩敵無戰吉風動旗幡不動
歌曰
> 風從旗幡不振揚 兩軍和合罷疆場
> 不必外使去通內 自有迴旗及故鄉

風雨相交必見陣當審風雲慢急隨之大勝風雲相交
歌曰

<column-break/>

旋風入屋室飛揚衣物當憂財物竊火及盜
凡行次逢來沖我宜回避暫止勿前
道路行 間有旋風逢來沖我宜回避候當方可向前
噫祥變無窮占盈雜註乃撮捷要為集註之篇舉宏綱為長
短之句壬午冬志欲匡國佑君之術志宜覽斯書按斯圖而
誦斯賦
->天文圖註祥異賦終<-

<vocab>行軍, 風雨, 旋風, 祥異, 旌籓, 天文, 占盈</vocab>

--- Page 311 ---
<language>Classical Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>

風向西方雲向東  詳其際慢審英雄
雲吉隨雲戰獲吉  風急隨風必有功

軍行無雨旗幡自濕者名陰露主兵懦弱不勝之兆濕旗埋王方吉

春東  夏南  秋西  冬北  歌曰

> 無風無雨旗幡濕  亦名陰露情悲泣
> 濕旗埋放王方中  拗取别色雜旗立

雨隨軍后向前者潤兵之雨其軍進獲大勝順軍之雨  歌曰

> 雨隨軍後軍勝强  敵人來敵必比傷
> 雨逆我軍軍且住  進前前見血流滂

臨陣彼軍上有雨我軍上晴天泣外軍之兆我宜獲時進計大勝

---

兩軍上雨暗晴  歌曰

> 彼軍上雨我軍晴  天泣他軍我軍晴
> 雨在我軍晴在彼  切宜牢固不可行

行軍過雨後有風後去有晴不恒此是散人冷軍雨改日規雨時不恒

歌曰

> 時風時雨暗復晴  天泣他軍我擘兵
> 若不陰謀别有提  火頭準備保軍城

夜出軍見月從東雲與月交者戰陣奉雲浸月隨雲戰吉雲月相

歌曰

> 雲月相交必戰爭  皆隨戰憂不能亭
> 雲急隨雲征戰吉  月歸東畔利西征

風飄家書營價値千金不要收之慎散之兆祭土地名書吉風飄

<vocab>旗幡, 陰露, 王方, 天泣, 戰陣, 征戰, 散人冷軍, 血流滂</vocab>

--- Page 312 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

月背有昏雨不雨更無光色主內吏陰私結黨連用寬察吉月背不雨
歌曰
> 陰沈不雨又無光 內吏無光細審詳
> 見陣人心又及覆 机謀主將好隄防

---

行軍見月中有黑如印大小忽 挫主陰人陽謀及軍情 歌曰
> 黑如印在月陰宮 三朝無雨即無殃
> 無雨且慎陰謹事 春在秋前夏候冬

---

行軍見旋風起左右前後有伏兵下殺陰人入營驚備見風起處出長刀手衝其方后 <gloss>時歸吉</gloss> 歌曰
> 旋風忽起在軍前 左右前後亦如然
> 防慎伏兵宜我將 三軍驚備自安全

<column-break/>

書物營 歌曰
> 營中雜物被風飄 飄書緣奇得息消
> 軍旅其星憂失喪 各為祀饗去推憂

---

風吹物入營大吉合得敵人土地封疆宜饗土地大勝風飄物入營
歌曰
> 風飄外物入軍營 進得封疆勇士真
> 神衛天然軍大吉 彼奸心怯有降聲

---

夜出戰月在將士影在東者宜東戰在西者宜西戰隨影而吉逆影而凶隨月影吉 <gloss>夜戰</gloss> 歌曰
> 欲戰東軍影在東 背他月戰必亨通
> 閱他西月或非吉 好審前來向北攻

<vocab>書物營, 風飄, 土地封疆, 軍大吉, 夜戰, 月背, 內吏陰私, 旋風, 伏兵, 三軍</vocab>

--- Page 313 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

->歌曰<-

> 虹飲前水敗乍縱 且暫回戈用散函
> 飲後江河休過去 方於江上渡亨通

虹飲營中水主流血荒虚將軍厄移營吉若不移營修補更改即凶

### 長虹飲營中水

->歌曰<-

> 長虹下飲營中水 見血灾傷病不已
> 修營更改用禳之 熏慎火光洗血所

虹飲人家及井池等此家先三破后生美女而寡婦九族

### 虹飲人家

->歌曰<-

> 如飲人家水不祥 初凶三破后还良
> 必生美女連一族 九族武蔭轉光揚

<column-break/>

行軍風吹樹木摧折攔前路者進雖摧折木攔后若向推詳此准備

### 伏兵風木攔路

->歌曰<-

> 摧木攔前前敵强 攔我軍後後遭殃
> 若再軍前進不易 或傷軍後退難當

軍行處虹攔前路當時鳴鑼吹角禳之待虹消散後方進發吉長

### 虹攔路

->歌曰<-

> 長虹横截軍前路 声鑼吹角軍前住
> 待虹散時軍方行 南北東西一任去

軍行見長虹在軍前飲水兵馬若役所飲水處凶却三七里住營營進仍於虹先飲水處别路過水即禍解吉

### 虹飲軍前水

<vocab>伏兵, 虹攔路, 鳴鑼吹角, 禳, 虹飲, 移營, 亨通, 熏慎, 武蔭</vocab>

--- Page 314 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

## 軍行旬日連陰不雨又無日光此主將士不安覆兼慎路上伏兵陰謀之兆 歌曰

> 連陰旬日雨全無 將士灾穀在道途
> 宮姓黃旗先引去 士能安得禍消除

出軍塵埃冥冥沾人衣上如泥點者應之軍灾宜住勿進戰凶如雨軍將有者兩解塵埃如泥沾人 歌曰

> 冥冥塵埃亦難開 沾在人身莫才
> 戰不勝兮宜兩解 不然秘計使通來

行軍及住營寨如遇土毛石血并諸色着宜屯住排戰不出吉<gloss>天雨土石</gloss> 歌曰

> 天雨諸狀總不祥 士憂灾入將憂殃

<column-break/>

### 行軍見疾別擇日 住應排戰出應亡

營內夜見兵鋒上有如星光明將至燈火前又不見者此為太白敗星先嬴后輸將軍有難取其星光內鋒截埋於歲利吉兵鋒夜光 歌曰

> 軍中鋒上夜如星 將至灯前又不明
> 先勝後敗必主准 截取埋了歲月利

軍中忽見刃劍鋒上有血者敗亡之灾連令各軍磨整器械大吉兵鋒有血 歌曰

> 血沾鋒瓣兵無利 亡弱將灾有七忌
> 磨整器械用厭了 更若興工為要秘

卓望旗倒憂見陣失利所倒旗鋒向處是禍灾哀蓊須牢固城

<vocab>軍行, 伏兵, 太白敗星, 土毛石血, 兵鋒, 灾殃</vocab>

--- Page 315 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>
<columns>2</columns>

若在别方牢固守　逢之戰陣不須為
思乱當以弓射之二箭其害凌光等不宜戰也
歌曰　水沸江翻民思乱　王命無通難理貫
見以此當令射之　法師自得消灾散
營寨内井泉沸憂陰謀盜賊起於正東上取青石一枚可重十斤已
用酒薰之数沸后投井泉禳　歌曰
井泉勝沸有陰凶　酒薰青石投沸中
依法速禳無灾慮　免憂内外有人通
營寨内井忽泉竭情轉弱憂暴兵相臨戰不勝埋生鉄五斤於井内
歌曰　井泉營寨内乾枯　切慮逢奸勢力輸

<column-break/>

纛若倒營寨當截取埋于寨門路踐踏而過殃難必除　歌曰
旗倒在地我勢强　倒來營寨我軍亡
旗鎗埋寨踐踏過　戰師逢此好思量
軍行及營寨不問四季雷在軍后鳴者兵得天助神祐進獲成功大
勝之兆在軍前鳴者宜屯左右不占　歌曰
出陣必然有勝殊　雷隨軍后振魁渠
若在軍前休戰敵　瘟疾流血并道途
軍行營寨去有雷霹靂物色若在大將軍行本命土地天冲神助
若在别方不宜出軍攻戰過一月方吉　歌曰
神雷霹靂助軍威　本年行年計可施

<vocab>營寨, 纛, 旗鎗, 雷霹靂, 井泉, 禳, 凌光, 戰陣, 神助, 神祐, 法師</vocab>

--- Page 316 ---
<language>Chinese</language>
<page-type>text</page-type>

五斤生鉄埋乾井　　不居營内已無虞

軍行營寨側進有樹木如湯火所迫此時将軍得時得天時助也

> 歌曰　排軍行戦契天机　　鉄卒雄師震大威
> 寇敢胆飛魂敗散　　更應後此有降師

營寨忽有樹木非時花及結實此軍交暴兵之准宜祈此埋於歳
月刑上其灾自除若自利各太歳本依也

> 歌曰　不是去時再發生　　只憂暴敗有陰雲
> 但能依経禳厭法　　祈此埋於歳月刑

竹無故開花居竹　　枯節

大輿血地若去不損竹　　天文祥異賦占行軍終

<vocab>行軍, 營寨, 禳厭法, 天文祥異賦, 乾井, 歳月刑, 大輿, 鉄卒</vocab>
